# Agents · M · part 7

963 entries, method-learnings-researcher to migration-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **method-learnings-researcher**: Searches docs/solutions/ for past learnings that apply to the current task, matched by frontmatter metadata. Use before implementing, deciding, or starting work in a documented area. - **method-maintainability-reviewer**: Always-on code-review persona. - **metis**: Pre-planning analyst. Detects contradictions, ambiguity, missing constraints, and execution risks in a draft plan or request before the planner commits. Read-only. Use when validating a plan draft or surfacing gaps before execution. ([cameleonh/omo-adaptive](https://github.com/cameleonh/omo-adaptive/blob/HEAD/packages/omo-claude/agents/metis.md))
- **metis**: Métis's workflow router. Turns a request into a defined workflow with ordered stages and explicit human gates, rather than a set of CLI verbs whose order somebody has to remember. ([catools2/metis](https://github.com/catools2/metis/blob/HEAD/plugins/metis/agents/metis.agent.md))
- **metis**: Read-only consultant that runs before plan generation. Surfaces ambiguities, hidden intentions, scope-creep risks, and unvalidated assumptions. Feeds directives back to Prometheus. ([erkandogan/sodium](https://github.com/erkandogan/sodium/blob/HEAD/agents/metis.md))
- **metis**: Test strategist. Owns TEST-STRATEGY and ORACLES from Kalchas inventory; plans risk-weighted coverage but does not execute tests, validate defects, or report final outcomes. ([holi87/holak-teams](https://github.com/holi87/holak-teams/blob/HEAD/argus/claude/agents/metis.md))
- **metis**: Metis — the resource surveyor of the Olympus family. Before a compute-heavy build or run, it investigates the ACTUAL execution environment — not nominal hardware but the EFFECTIVE limits the process really has (container/cgroup CPU & memory caps, Kubernetes pod limits, SLURM allocation, CPU affinity, GPU, disk/scratch, BLAS threading) — and hands the coding agent a concrete resource budget so generated code fits the machine instead of over-subscribing cores or getting OOM-killed. Probes read-only; advises, does not write code. Reports to Hephaestus (who builds to the budget) and Zeus; distinc… ([MaleenKidiwela/Olympus](https://github.com/MaleenKidiwela/Olympus/blob/HEAD/plugins/olympus/agents/metis.md))
- **metis**: PROACTIVELY research codebase context and return structured findings to a parent agent. MUST BE USED when a parent agent needs deep subsystem analysis, pattern discovery, or implementation option evaluation. Automatically invoke for tasks requiring understanding of how an existing subsystem works, what patterns to follow, or what files/functions are involved. Returns structured findings — never writes plans or implements code. Can spawn Hermes for file discovery and Athena for domain validation. ([MikeBlakeway/tribes](https://github.com/MikeBlakeway/tribes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/metis.md))
- **metis**: Pre-planning consultant that analyzes requests to identify hidden intentions, ambiguities, and AI failure points. ([nicholls-inc/claude-agent-kit](https://github.com/nicholls-inc/claude-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/metis.md))
- **metis**: Use when a problem needs structured analysis or an under-specified assignment needs a proposed solution before any code is written — a GitHub issue/PR number or URL, a JIRA key/URL, a Bugsnag error, a described failure, or the current task context. Runs the analyze-problem framework, proposes the smallest safe solution, and publishes a reusable plan artifact as a GitHub issue, then hands back an "Analysis done" handoff with links. Read-only — never edits, commits, pushes, or implements. ([pekral/phpstan-rules](https://github.com/pekral/phpstan-rules/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/metis.md))
- **metis**: Pre-planning consultant — analyzes requests BEFORE planning to surface hidden intent, ambiguities, AI-slop risks; generates clarifying questions and directives for Prometheus. Read-only. Ported from OMO agents/metis.ts METIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT (OMP-adapted). ([sharosoo/dotfile](https://github.com/sharosoo/dotfile/blob/HEAD/omp/omo-layer/agents/metis.md))
- **metis**: Pre-planning analyst. Detects contradictions, ambiguity, missing constraints, and execution risks in a draft plan or request before the planner commits. Read-only. ([younghai/lazyz](https://github.com/younghai/lazyz/blob/HEAD/plugins/lazyz/agents/metis.md) · 2 copies)
- **Metis Arbiter**: L4 terminal leaf - ARBITER. Independent decision-maker for forks the loop cannot resolve on its own. Under UNATTENDED mode it ALWAYS rules and continues, NEVER escalates to the user. Output is a binding ruling logged to the ledger. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-arbiter.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Coordinator**: Orchestrates complex multi-step tasks by breaking them down into disjoint lanes and delegating to specialist subagents (planner, implementer, reviewer, verifier, depth-prober, juror, goal-checker). ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-coordinator.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Depth-prober**: L4 terminal leaf - G3.5 DEPTH-LOCK. Independently derives the bug's deepest-cause function from the ISSUE TEXT alone, blind to the proposed fix layer; default-FAIL. Emits D1-D5. depth-miss REJECTs to G1. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-depth-prober.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Execharness-resolver**: L3 executor - EXECHARNESS RESOLVE. Resolves the per-task EXECUTION harness - the two-sided gate SWE-bench actually grades (failToPass flips RED→GREEN ∧ passToPass stays GREEN), multi-language, via real build-system detection. Ingests shipped FAIL_TO_PASS/PASS_TO_PASS, else derives failToPass from the mission's behavioral acceptance asks. An unresolvable environment is BLOCKED, never a stand-in. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-execharness-resolver.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Feature-coordinator**: L1 feature coordinator - drives approved ROADMAP.md lanes through their required build/review/verification gates and owns the run-wide feature frontier. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-feature-coordinator.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Framework-generator**: L3 executor - FRAMEWORK GENERATE. When the SELECTOR returns MISS, generates a one-off custom framework for the exact task shape - classifies the orthogonal axes, composes the gate sequence from the GATE-LIBRARY with the correct axis-specific gate, emits the gen-&lt;axis-signature&gt; leaf with the BLOCKED invariant verbatim, binds an execharness, and hands it to the validator before any gate runs. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-framework-generator.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Framework-validator**: L4 terminal leaf - FRAMEWORK VALIDATE (HRN-5). A fresh, default-FAIL juror that proves a GENERATED framework is SOUND before any gate runs. Checks the HRN-5 default-FAIL checklist - every gate mapped, exactly one terminal DONE with negatives looping UP, the BLOCKED invariant verbatim, a non-empty acceptance set. PASS lets the leaf be driven; FAIL with numbered reasons returns it to the generator. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-framework-validator.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Fresh-verifier**: L4 blind fresh verifier - independently checks a candidate roadmap or plan against the exact mission and repository; APPROVE/REJECT, default-FAIL. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-fresh-verifier.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Goal-checker**: L4 terminal leaf - GOAL-CHECK. Independent, adversarial, default-FAIL. Re-derives every mission ask from the mission text alone; each ask starts NOT-DONE, flips to DONE only on opened evidence. DONE only if zero open findings at ANY severity AND user-usable AND (coverage &gt;=95% only when the admitted oracle is code-behavior TDD) AND a tri-axis end-to-end run (scope + original prompt + potential flaws) is on record. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-goal-checker.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Implementer**: L3 executor - G4 IMPLEMENT. Builds one feature from its approved executable roadmap item or conditional frozen plan using strict TDD and real test runs; coverage &gt;=95% on changed lines when the admitted oracle is code-behavior TDD. Reports PLAN-CONFLICT rather than improvising. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-implementer.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Intake**: L3 legacy-resume compatibility reader - reconstructs old intake artifacts when explicitly resuming them; new runs use the useful-first roadmap author instead. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-intake.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Janitor**: L4 terminal leaf - JANITOR. Writes the DONE sentinel atomically and removes only scratch artifacts after the three-file governance state and substantive evidence pass validation. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-janitor.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Juror**: L4 terminal leaf - G7 SIGN-OFF. One independent sign-off panel seat that saw none of the intermediate work. Binary PASS/FAIL on opened evidence; default-FAIL. A FAIL naming a P0/P1 blocker is NOT arbitrable into PASS. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-juror.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Manager**: L2 optional manager - coordinates a multi-lane slice, builds compact pointer envelopes, and dispatches disjoint L3 work without executing it. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-manager.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Planner**: L3 conditional G1 planner - adds detail only when a roadmap item explicitly requires it, including debug depth-lock and unresolved design forks. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-planner.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Preflight-probe**: L4 diagnostic/recovery probe - on an explicit cache miss, proves RUN/READ/WRITE and reports model/effort bindings; never the mandatory first spawn. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-preflight-probe.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Re-anchor**: L4 terminal leaf - RE-ANCHOR. Confirms mission and roadmap frontier alignment after resume or compaction using the three-file governance state. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-re-anchor.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Researcher**: L3 executor - bounded research that materializes a usable output with reconciled receipts. Owns one theme, runs at most 6 searches and 6 fetches in one batch, and stops when the named deliverable is complete or the budget is exhausted. Does not spawn. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-researcher.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Reviewer**: L3 independent G2/G5 or roadmap reviewer - checks mission coverage, reality, tests, boundaries, and claim-vs-diff; returns binary SMASH or PASS. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-reviewer.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Scope-coordinator**: L1 scope coordinator - drives the useful-first adaptive roadmap flow and returns one independently approved executable ROADMAP.md before build. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-scope-coordinator.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Scoper**: L3 useful-first roadmap author or complementary scout - proves capability when needed, inspects the real repository, and contributes to one executable ROADMAP.md without spawning. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-scoper.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Scribe**: L4 terminal scribe - records new-run governance in PROMPTS.txt, ROADMAP.md, and append-only GATELOG.md; preserves legacy ledgers read-only. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-scribe.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Sweep-coordinator**: L1 sweep coordinator - drives independent convergence, goal checking, and cleanup from the three-file ledger plus substantive evidence. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-sweep-coordinator.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Sweeper**: L3 executor - SWEEP. Fresh production-readiness sweeper that re-derives mission coverage, inspects the changed neighborhood, checks GATELOG provenance, and returns evidence-backed P0..P3 findings. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-sweeper.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Synthesizer**: L3 roadmap synthesizer/repair author - merges retained scout evidence into the one canonical executable ROADMAP.md and repairs only rejected items; does not spawn. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-synthesizer.md) · ★444)
- **Metis Verifier**: L3 independent G6 verifier - proves behavior with real before/after runs, regression checks, adversarial inputs, and &gt;=95% changed-line coverage. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-verifier.md) · ★444)
- **metis-accessibility-reviewer**: Review semantics, keyboard access, focus, labels, contrast, and error communication. ([gkrtjd99/Metis](https://github.com/gkrtjd99/Metis/blob/HEAD/adapters/opencode/.opencode/agents/metis-accessibility-reviewer.md) · 4 copies)
- **metis-adversarial-reviewer**: Assume the completion candidate is wrong and search for hidden failure. ([gkrtjd99/Metis](https://github.com/gkrtjd99/Metis/blob/HEAD/adapters/opencode/.opencode/agents/metis-adversarial-reviewer.md) · 4 copies)

- **metis-behavior-modeling**: Check a state machine for determinism, guard completeness, reachability and observability before anything is generated from it, and report unverifiable guards as the third outcome they are rather than as a pass. ([catools2/metis](https://github.com/catools2/metis/blob/HEAD/plugins/metis/agents/metis-behavior-modeling.agent.md))
- **metis-business-analyst**: Analyse a stated intent before it reaches the graph — reading it from four directions, consolidating what is missing, and refusing the import only when the claim cannot be represented honestly. ([catools2/metis](https://github.com/catools2/metis/blob/HEAD/plugins/metis/agents/metis-business-analyst.agent.md))
- **metis-change-impact**: Show which recovered behaviour a change touches and what validates it, then take the change to the approval gate — re-recovering, revoking only the approvals whose behaviour moved, and stopping for a human on those. ([catools2/metis](https://github.com/catools2/metis/blob/HEAD/plugins/metis/agents/metis-change-impact.agent.md))
- **metis-code-explorer**: Investigate one question against the existing codebase. Returns an inline report; writes nothing. ([gsaranti/pantheon](https://github.com/gsaranti/pantheon/blob/HEAD/plugins/metis/.claude-code/agents/metis-code-explorer.md) · ★3)

- **metis-coordinator**: Coordinate one bounded subtree and return compact synthesis. ([gkrtjd99/Metis_Legacy](https://github.com/gkrtjd99/Metis_Legacy/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/agents/coordinator.md) · 2 copies)
